1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The reinforcement variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 510 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 80 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 510 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the reinforcement structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 80. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
